## Neonatal outcomes following open versus minimally invasive surgery for duodenal atresia: a propensity score matched analysis.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 美国
- 通讯作者: Castro P (pcastro@uchc.edu)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163302
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163302) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42480712/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：通过倾向评分匹配分析，比较新生儿十二指肠闭锁修复术中开放手术与微创手术的临床结局。
- 核心发现/数据：匹配后，微创手术组中位手术时间显著长于开放组（166分钟 vs 111分钟，p<0.001）；两组在复合并发症发生率（12.1% vs 18.5%，p=0.08）、再入院率（1.0% vs 0.5%）、再干预率（4.4% vs 2.9%）及术后住院>30天比例（32.0% vs 40.3%）方面均无显著差异。研究期间微创手术的使用率显著增加（p=0.006）。
- 临床启示：对于合适的患儿，微创手术可作为开放手术的安全替代方案，但需注意其手术时间更长，对麻醉和手术团队有更高要求。

**原文摘要**：This study aims to evaluate outcomes for neonates undergoing open versus minimally invasive surgery (MIS) for duodenal atresia repair using a propensity score matched cohort from a national database. Neonates undergoing duodenal atresia repair from 2016-2023 were identified using the National Surgical Quality Improvement Program-Pediatric (NSQIP-P) database. Baseline characteristics of each group were matched using propensity scores. Outcomes were compared using an intention-to-treat analysis. Pearson's chi-square, Fisher's exact, and Mann-Whitney's U tests were used as appropriate. Annual operative trends were assessed using a Cochran-Armitage trend test. A total of 1,485 neonates were identified who underwent duodenal atresia repair. Pre-match, neonates who underwent open repair were smaller, more often premature, required more ventilator support, more frequently required emergent interventions, and had higher ASA class compared to MIS. There were 206 neonates included in each group after propensity score matching (PSM). These groups were similar in baseline characteristics. Post-match, MIS repair was associated with a longer median operative time (open 111 mins, IQR 87-143 vs MIS 166 mins, IQR 127-206, p<0.001). There were no differences in composite morbidity (open 18.5% vs MIS 12.1%, p=0.08), readmission (open 0.5% vs MIS 1.0%, p=0.56), reinterventions (open 2.9% vs MIS 4.4%, p=0.43), or postoperative admission lasting >30 days (open 40.3% vs MIS 32.0%, p=0.08). MIS utilization increased significantly over the study period (p=0.006). In this matched cohort, MIS was associated with similar outcomes as the open approach, with the exception of longer operative times. MIS repair of duodenal atresia has steadily increased over the study period. Retrospective comparative study LEVEL OF EVIDENCE: Level III.

---

## Locum Tenens Coverage and Access to Pediatric Surgical Care in the United States.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: Lexington
- 通讯作者: Martin AE (aemartin55@hotmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163300
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163300) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42476218/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估美国儿科外科实践中临时替班（Locum Tenens）外科医生的普及率、实践环境及其对临床覆盖范围的影响，为劳动力规划与可及性策略提供依据。  
- 核心发现/数据：在248个儿科外科实践中，23.8%（59个）使用了LT外科医生；LT实践的规模显著更小（中位2名 vs 4名外科医生，p<0.001），且较少设有专科培训项目（8.5% vs 24.9%，p=0.006）。LT支持使患者中位旅行距离几乎减半（跨州从48.6英里降至29.2英里，州内从51.5英里降至31.3英里），距离手术护理超过60英里的儿童比例从19.9%降至6.7%（受影响人数从约1450万降至约500万）。  
- 临床启示：LT外科医生在较小、非培训型实践中关键性地扩大了儿童外科护理可及性并缩短了旅行距离，但其依赖性也暴露了劳动力脆弱性，需在公平医疗保障规划中予以重视。

**原文摘要**：Workforce shortages and geography may limit access to pediatric surgical care. Locum tenens (LT) surgeons help fill staffing gaps, yet their role in pediatric surgery remains poorly defined. This study evaluates the prevalence, practice settings, and clinical coverage of LT pediatric surgeons to inform workforce planning and access strategies. The American Pediatric Surgical Association (APSA) membership list was used to identify United States pediatric surgery practices. Each practice was contacted to obtain data on surgeon composition, use of LT, advanced practice providers (APPs) composition, and the number of hospitals, clinics, consultation sites, and operating rooms covered as of July 31, 2025. Service locations were geocoded by zip code. Values are expressed as median and interquartile range [IQR]. Comparisons between practices using and not using LT were done by Mann-Whitney U tests, with p<0.05 considered significant. Of 248 practices, 59 (23.8%) reported using LT. LT practices are in 29 of 50 states and the District of Columbia (56.9%), most commonly in Florida (n=6), Texas (n=5), New Jersey, and Pennsylvania (n=4 each). LT surgeons cover a median of 1 [1-1] hospital. Compared with non-LT (NLT) practices, groups using LT were significantly smaller (median 2 [2-3] vs. 4 [3-6.5] surgeons, p < 0.001) and less likely to host a fellowship (8.5% vs. 24.9%, p = 0.006). They covered fewer hospitals, clinics, consultation sites, and operating sites (all p < 0.01). APP employment and practices that cross state lines were similar between groups. Maintaining LT-supported practices substantially improves access, reducing median travel distances by almost half (from 48.6 to 29.2 miles across state lines and from 51.5 to 31.3 miles within state lines). The proportion of children living >60 miles from surgical care dropped from 19.9% to 6.7%, reducing the number affected from ∼14.5 million to about 5 million. LT surgeons play a critical role in pediatric surgery, expanding access and reducing travel distances for children, particularly in smaller, non-fellowship practices. While LT reliance helps fill gaps, it also highlights potential workforce vulnerabilities. Understanding LT utilization is essential for planning a resilient pediatric surgical workforce and ensuring equitable care nationwide.

---

## Vacuum-Assisted Mini-PCNL Versus RIRS with flexible and navigable suction ureteral access sheath for 1-3 cm Pediatric Stones: A Comparative Study.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 中国 · The Fifth Affiliated Hospital of Guangzhou Medical University
- 通讯作者: Yang W (vgyang@qq.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163313
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163313) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42471090/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：比较真空辅助迷你经皮肾镜取石术（VA-mini PCNL）与使用柔性可导航吸引输尿管通道鞘的逆行输尿管软镜手术（FANS-RIRS）治疗1-3 cm儿童上尿路结石的效果。
- 核心发现/数据：两组结石清除率（97.3% vs. 91.3%，p=0.552）、手术时间（95 vs. 85分钟，p=0.398）和并发症发生率（2.7% vs. 4.35%，p=1）无显著差异。VA-mini PCNL组住院时间更长（9 vs. 7天，p=0.001），但费用更低（1921.50 vs. 2339.68美元，p<0.001）。
- 临床启示：两种术式均为治疗1-3 cm儿童上尿路结石的有效选择；VA-mini PCNL更具成本效益，而FANS-RIRS可缩短住院时间。

**原文摘要**：Compare vacuum-assisted mini-percutaneous nephrolithotomy (VA-mini PCNL) and RIRS with flexible and navigable suction ureteral access sheath (FANS-RIRS) for pediatric upper urinary tract stones. This retrospective study included children under 14 years old with 1-3 cm stones treated from March 2021 to June 2024. Patients matched for demographics and stone characteristics. Primary outcomes included: stone-free rate (SFR), complications, lab parameters, operative time, hospitalization cost and duration. There were 37 children in the VA-mini PCNL group (16 Fr sheath) and 23 in the FANS-RIRS group (10/12 Fr sheath). In the FANS-RIRS group, 34.78% underwent preoperative stent placement. No significant differences were found between groups in SFR (97.3% vs. 91.3%, p=0.552), surgical time (95 vs. 85 minutes, p=0.398), or complication rate (2.7% vs. 4.35%, p=1). Complications included fever in VA-mini PCNL and perirenal hematoma in FANS-RIRS. Stone baskets were used only in FANS-RIRS (21.74%, p=0.006). The VA-mini PCNL group had an 81.1% tubeless rate. Neither surgery significantly affected creatinine, blood urea nitrogen (BUN), or hemoglobin levels (p>0.05). Postoperative white blood cell counts increased in both groups (p=0.003 and p=0.039, respectively), with no significant difference between groups (p=0.331). VA-mini PCNL had a longer hospital stay (9 vs. 7 days, p=0.001) but lower costs ($1921.50 vs. $2339.68) , p<0.001). Both techniques are viable options for pediatric upper urinary tract stones of 1-3 cm.VA-mini PCNL is more cost-effective, while FANS-RIRS offers a shorter hospital stay.

---

## Volvulus in preterm neonates: An overlooked diagnosis.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: London · Evelina London Children Hospital
- 通讯作者: Khodary AR (ahmedrefaatkhodary@gmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163314
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163314) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42471089/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：比较早产儿与足月儿肠扭转的术前表现、诊断路径、手术管理及术后结局，强调早产儿肠扭转常被漏诊或误诊。
- 核心发现/数据：早产儿出现胆汁性呕吐的比例显著低于足月儿（p=0.015），而腹胀（p=0.02）和全身状况恶化更常见，初始临床印象多倾向坏死性小肠结肠炎（NEC）。早产儿从转诊到手术的中位时间更长（12小时 vs. 2.5小时），肠切除率更高（66.6% vs. 44.4%），且术后并发症显著更多（p<0.01）、达到全肠内喂养时间更长（p=0.010）、住院时间更长（p=0.007）。
- 临床启示：早产儿肠扭转因表现不典型极易被误诊为NEC，导致手术延迟和更高的并发症率及死亡率，临床医生需提高警惕，对出现腹胀和全身恶化的早产儿应尽早考虑肠扭转可能。

**原文摘要**：Intestinal volvulus is a potentially devastating condition where prompt surgical management can avert significant mortality and morbidity. However, the diagnosis in preterm infants can be challenging. We aimed to compare preoperative presentation, diagnostic pathways, surgical management and postoperative outcomes between preterm and full-term infants with intestinal volvulus. All neonatal patients with intestinal volvulus confirmed on laparotomy in the two year period October 2023 to September 2025 were identified using the electronic patient record. Patients were categorized into Group A (preterm, <37 weeks) and Group B (full-term, ≥37 weeks). Data including demographics, age at presentation, reason for referral, time to surgery, and postoperative course were extracted from the electronic patient record and compared. Eighteen infants were included (9 per group). Premature infants were significantly less likely to present with bilious vomiting (p=0.015) and more likely to present with abdominal distension (p=0.02) and generalized deterioration. Initial clinical impression favoured necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) in preterms. Preterm infants had a longer median time from referral to surgery (12 vs. 2.5 hours) and higher intestinal resection rates (66.6% vs. 44.4%), though these did not reach statistical significance. Preterm infants experienced significantly more postoperative complications (p<0.01), longer times to full feeds (p=0.010), and longer hospital stays (p=0.007). Volvulus in preterm infants is frequently misidentified as NEC due to atypical presentation. This diagnostic challenge leads to delays in surgical intervention and is associated with significantly higher morbidity and mortality compared to term infants.

## Using rapid-sequence MRI scans to compare ventricular and intracranial volumes in shunted and non-shunted children with myelomeningocele: a retrospective observational cohort study.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Childs Nerv Syst
- 国家/单位: 美国 · University of Virginia
- 通讯作者: Spader H (hls3e@uvahealth.org)
- DOI: 10.1007/s00381-026-07410-w
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00381-026-07410-w) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498836/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：比较脊髓脊膜膨出患儿中接受与未接受脑室腹腔分流术者的颅内及脑室容积差异。
- 核心发现/数据：分流组患儿基线颅内容积大于非分流组（644.3 vs 403.5 mL，p=0.020），但脑室容积差异不显著（82.2 vs 34.2 mL，p=0.072）；50天内分流组脑室/颅内容积比显著更高（p=0.026），校正头围和早产后无显著差异（p=0.144）；效应量分析显示50天内差异大（Hedges' g=1.268），后减小至0.452。
- 临床启示：分流治疗的MMC患儿在新生儿期存在颅内容积增大和相对脑室扩大，但这种差异在50天后消失，提示早期脑室扩大是暂时性的，不必据此过早判断分流必要性。

**原文摘要**：Hydrocephalus is a common complication of myelomeningocele (MMC), typically managed with a ventriculoperitoneal shunt (VPS). Despite clinical relevance, there remains a paucity of studies quantitatively comparing intracranial and ventricular volumes in shunted versus non-shunted MMC patients. This study aims to determine whether significant volumetric differences exist between these groups. Ninety-one MRIs from 19 MMC patients (13 shunted, 6 non-shunted), aged 0-3 years, were retrospectively analyzed. Volumes were obtained from rapid-sequence MRIs using automatic segmentation with manual correction. Outcomes included ventricular volume, intracranial volume, and ventricle-to-intracranial volume (ventricle/ICV) ratio. Analyses included nonparametric tests, mixed-effects models, bootstrap resampling, permutation testing, and robust regression. At baseline, shunted patients exhibited larger intracranial volumes (644.3 vs 403.5 mL; p = 0.020) but not ventricular volumes (82.2 vs 34.2 mL; p = 0.072) compared to non-shunted patients. Although the ventricle-to-intracranial volume ratio was significantly higher in shunted patients under 50 days of age (p = 0.026), this difference was no longer significant after adjusting for head circumference and prematurity (p = 0.144). Sensitivity analyses confirmed differences in the neonatal period (p = 0.013), but not in infancy or toddlerhood. Effect-size analyses demonstrated a large difference within 50 days (Hedges' g = 1.268) that diminished thereafter (g = 0.452). Shunted MMC patients demonstrated larger intracranial volumes and higher ventricle/ICV ratios in the neonatal period, but these differences diminished after 50 days. Early ventricular enlargement in this cohort's shunted patients appears robust yet transient, with no sustained volumetric differences between shunted and non-shunted patients later in development.

## Newborn screening for external congenital anomalies at three public hospitals in Karachi, Pakistan.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: BMJ Paediatr Open · 中科院医学4区(儿科4区) · IF 2.6 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 新加坡
- 通讯作者: Roy N (nobsroy@gmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1136/bmjpo-2025-004015
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1136/bmjpo-2025-004015) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498489/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：通过系统性新生儿筛查，评估巴基斯坦卡拉奇三家公立医院外部先天性畸形的患病率及相关新生儿死亡率。
- 核心发现/数据：在25,414例新生儿中筛查了18,728例（73.7%），发现重大外部畸形334例（1.8%），轻微畸形3384例（18.1%）；最常见重大畸形包括马蹄内翻足、尿道下裂、唇腭裂和脊柱裂；伴有重大畸形的新生儿在新生儿期死亡率（23.0%）显著高于轻微畸形（1.8%）和无畸形组（1.3%）。
- 临床启示：由经过培训的非医师卫生工作者进行新生儿外部畸形筛查可行且可推广，但必须配套有效的转诊和护理路径以降低死亡率与并发症。

**原文摘要**：Reliable prevalence data on congenital anomalies are lacking in low- and middle-income countries (LMICs), including Pakistan. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of external congenital anomalies through systematic newborn screening (NBS) in public sector hospitals in Karachi, Pakistan. A cross-sectional NBS programme was implemented from July 2023 to June 2024. Health workers (HWs) conducted head-to-toe newborn examinations, documented anomalies and provided counselling and referral guidance. Rates of intrauterine deaths and pre- and post-discharge neonatal mortalities were documented. Of the 25 414 births in the study period, 18 728 (73.7%) were screened. Major external anomalies were identified in 334 births (1.8%) and minor anomalies in 3384 (18.1%). The most common major anomalies included clubfoot, hypospadias, cleft lip/palate and spina bifida. Of all neonates screened, follow-up at the end of the neonatal period was completed for 70.6%, with 23.0% deaths recorded for those with major anomalies as compared to 1.8% with minor anomalies and 1.3% with no identified anomalies. Preterm birth, male sex and study site were significantly associated with the presence of anomalies ( This is the largest systematically conducted NBS for external congenital anomalies in Pakistan, highlighting a substantial burden of congenital anomalies and associated neonatal mortality. Screening by trained non-physician HWs is feasible and scalable. However, screening must be paired with effective referral and care pathways to reduce mortality and morbidity.

## Serum PLA2R-negative, biopsy-proven PLA2R-positive primary membranous nephropathy in a dichorionic-diamniotic twin pregnancy.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: BMJ Case Rep · 中科院医学4区(医学：内科4区) · IF 0.4
- 国家/单位: Victoria
- 通讯作者: Kansal A (abhik.kansal@gmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1136/bcr-2026-272147
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1136/bcr-2026-272147) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498317/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报告一例双绒毛膜双羊膜囊双胎妊娠中血清PLA2R阴性但肾活检PLA2R阳性的原发性膜性肾病病例，探讨其诊断与治疗挑战。
- 核心发现/数据：患者在孕20+6周表现为肾病综合征，孕21周肾活检确诊PLA2R阳性及IgG4染色阳性，但血清PLA2R抗体阴性；经他克莫司和利妥昔单抗治疗后，于孕33+3周因胎膜早破行紧急剖宫产，分娩存活双胎，母婴结局良好。
- 临床启示：妊娠期血清阴性原发性膜性肾病诊断困难，肾活检具有重要作用；对于选择性患者，利妥昔单抗治疗可改善母婴预后。

**原文摘要**：Primary membranous nephropathy (PMN) in pregnancy is rare and poses significant risks including pre-eclampsia, preterm delivery, low birth weight and fetal death. Anti-phospholipase A2 receptor (PLA2R) antibodies are used to confirm diagnosis.We report a case of biopsy-confirmed PLA2R-positive but seronegative PMN in a woman in her late 20s with a dichorionic-diamniotic twin pregnancy. Initial hypoalbuminaemia was attributed to intercurrent infection but was later recognised as nephrotic syndrome at 20+6 weeks gestation. Renal biopsy at 21 weeks confirmed PMN with positive PLA2R and IgG4 staining, despite negative serum PLA2R antibodies.She was managed with tacrolimus and rituximab, with close monitoring and delivered viable twins via emergency caesarean at 33+3 weeks following premature rupture of membranes. Both maternal disease and neonatal outcomes were favourable. This case highlights the challenges in diagnosing and managing seronegative PMN during pregnancy and supports the role of renal biopsy and rituximab in selected patients.

## Maternal and fetal treatment outcomes of pregnancy-related aneurysmal subarachnoid hemorrhage: a nationwide study.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Neurosurg · 中科院医学2区(临床神经病学2区/外科2区) · IF 3.8 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: Helsinki · University of Helsinki and Helsinki University Hospital
- 通讯作者: Korja M
- DOI: 10.3171/2026.2.JNS252575
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.3171/2026.2.JNS252575) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42497459/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探讨妊娠期动脉瘤性蛛网膜下腔出血（aSAH）的母体及胎儿治疗结局。  
- 核心发现/数据：1987–2016年间芬兰全国28例住院孕产妇中，75%接受手术夹闭，14%行血管内治疗，11%两者联合，82%的干预在分娩前完成；所有住院孕产妇的婴儿均存活，无住院期间母胎死亡，但另有3例院前猝死。住院患者中29%为主动吸烟者，动脉瘤最大平均直径7.1 mm，18%发生血管痉挛，11%出现迟发性脑缺血，3个月中位改良Rankin量表评分为1。  
- 临床启示：妊娠期aSAH的住院母胎死亡率极低，所有死亡均发生于院前；妊娠相关的免疫调节可能对aSAH预后有潜在益处，值得进一步研究。

**原文摘要**：There is a paucity of data concerning the outcomes of aneurysmal subarachnoid hemorrhage (aSAH) in pregnant women. In this study, the authors investigated the clinical progression, treatment, and outcomes in women and their unborn children after aSAH during pregnancy. This nationwide retrospective population-based cohort study in Finland spans the years 1987-2016. The authors identified pregnant women diagnosed with aSAH through the Care Register for Health Care and Medical Birth Register (1,773,728 deliveries) and sudden prehospital deaths caused by aSAH from the Cause of Death Register. The authors reviewed medical records to delineate the clinical trajectories of each woman with aSAH. Twenty-eight pregnant women were hospitalized with aSAH between 1987 and 2016. In addition, 3 pregnant women died suddenly of aSAH at home or in the emergency department before hospitalization. Eight (29%) of the 28 hospitalized pregnant women were active smokers. The maximum mean aneurysm diameter was 7.1 (SD 4.2) mm. The aneurysm was surgically treated in 21 (75%) of the 28 women, endovascularly in 4 (14%), and by both methods in 3 (11%) patients. Most procedures (n = 23, 82%) were performed before childbirth. The most common method of delivery was emergency cesarean section (CS) (n = 13, 46%) followed by elective CS (n = 8, 29%). All infants of the hospitalized women survived. Vasospasm occurred in 5 (18%) and delayed cerebral ischemia in 3 (11%) women. There were no maternal or fetal deaths during hospitalization. The median modified Rankin Scale score at 3 months was 1 (IQR 0-2) for the hospitalized women. Two (7%) women received ventriculoperitoneal shunts for post-aSAH hydrocephalus. Aneurysmal SAH during pregnancy is associated with exceptionally low maternal and neonatal mortality. All maternal and fetal fatalities were due to sudden prehospital deaths. Pregnancy-related immunological modulation and its potential benefits on aSAH outcomes represent a compelling topic for future research.

## Effects of different oral intake management during labor on maternal and neonatal outcomes: A protocol for a systematic review and network meta-analysis.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: PLoS One · 中科院综合性期刊3区(综合性期刊3区) · IF 2.8 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 中国 · West China Second University Hospital
- 通讯作者: Chen X
- DOI: 10.1371/journal.pone.0354489
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0354489) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42497129/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：通过网络Meta分析确定分娩期间产妇口服摄入管理的最佳干预策略。
- 核心发现/数据：系统检索11个数据库至2026年1月，纳入所有评估分娩期口服摄入干预的随机对照试验；主要结局包括阴道分娩率、器械助产、剖宫产、10分钟Apgar评分和新生儿低血糖；次要结局涵盖产妇产程时长、酮症酸中毒、低血糖、呕吐率等，以及新生儿胎儿窘迫、黄疸、脐血pH<7.2和NICU入院。
- 临床启示：本研究将为分娩期是否禁食或限制口服摄入提供循证依据，帮助临床医生选择最优管理方案以改善母婴结局。

**原文摘要**：Fasting or restricting oral intake during labor may adversely affect maternal and neonatal outcomes. Although several interventions regarding oral intake during labor have been proposed, the optimal strategy for clinical practice remains controversial. This study aims to conduct a network meta-analysis to identify the most effective intervention for managing maternal oral intake during labor. A comprehensive search will be conducted in the following electronic databases: Web of Science, PubMed, Embase, Ovid, Cochrane Library, ClinicalTrials.gov, the WHO International Clinical Trials Registry Platform, the Chinese Biomedical Literature Database, China National Knowledge Infrastructure, Wanfang Database, and VIP Database, from database inception until January 2026. All randomized controlled trials (RCTs) evaluating oral intake interventions during labor will be included. The primary outcomes include vaginal delivery rate, operative vaginal delivery, caesarean section, 10-minute Apgar score, and neonatal hypoglycemia. Secondary outcomes include maternal and neonatal outcomes. Maternal outcomes include duration of labor, ketoacidosis, maternal hypoglycemia, postpartum hemorrhage, vomiting rate, Mendelson's syndrome, maternal thirst and hunger sensations, and maternal satisfaction. Neonatal outcomes include fetal distress, neonatal jaundice, umbilical cord blood pH < 7.2, and admission to the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU). Study selection, data extraction, and quality assessment will be conducted independently by two reviewers, and disagreements will be resolved through discussion with a third reviewer. Meta-analysis will be performed using RevMan 5.4 where appropriate; otherwise, subgroup analyses or descriptive analyses will be conducted. This review will not involve individual patient data and therefore does not require ethical approval. The findings of this systematic review and network meta-analysis will be disseminated through publication in a peer-reviewed journal and presentation at relevant academic conferences. CRD42025630953.

## Neuroprognostication in infants born preterm at the limits of viability: assessment under uncertainty.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Pediatr Res · 中科院医学3区(儿科2区) · IF 3.3 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 加拿大 · The Hospital for Sick Children
- 通讯作者: Cizmeci MN (mehmet.cizmeci@sickkids.ca)
- DOI: 10.1038/s41390-026-05331-7
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41390-026-05331-7) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498830/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：提出一种基于纵向临床推理的结构化方法，用于对生存极限早产儿进行神经预后评估，以应对早期预后高度不确定性的挑战。
- 核心发现/数据：生存极限早产儿虽占少数，但长期神经发育障碍比例极高；预后评估需整合临床、神经影像和环境风险因素，形成基线预后意见，并随NICU住院期间新出现的重大并发症（如支气管肺发育不良、坏死性小肠结肠炎、败血症）和脑损伤（如生发基质-脑室内出血、白质损伤、小脑出血）动态更新；预后应作为概率谱系而非二元预测。
- 临床启示：临床咨询应避免非好即坏的二元预测，强调概率性结局和不确定性，通过结构化框架促进透明风险沟通、共享决策，并为早期发育干预提供依据。

**原文摘要**：Infants born extremely preterm at the limits of viability (LoV) represent a relatively small population but account for a disproportionate share of long-term neurodevelopmental impairment. Clinical decision-making at the LoV is often guided by expectations of neurodevelopmental outcome, yet during these early days prognostic uncertainty is high. This review proposes a structured approach to neuroprognostication based on longitudinal clinical reasoning rather than single-timepoint prognostication. Being born at the LoV establishes baseline vulnerability through global brain dysmaturation. Subsequent prematurity-related clinical events, including major systemic morbidities such as bronchopulmonary dysplasia, necrotizing enterocolitis, and sepsis, progressively modify potential outcomes over time. Neonatal intensive care exposures, growth trajectory, environmental factors, and sex further influence developmental pathways. The presence of preterm brain injury, including germinal-matrix and intraventricular hemorrhage, white matter injury, and cerebellar hemorrhage is also an important predictor of neurodevelopmental outcome. Prognostic confidence emerges from integrating these data as new information becomes available. The proposed framework for neuroprognostication in infants born at the LoV integrates clinical, neuroimaging and environmental risk factors to form a prognostic opinion. Emphasis is placed on probabilistic outcomes, where the initial prognostic opinion serves as a baseline that can be updated as new clinical and neuroimaging information emerges. IMPACT: Neuroprognostication for infants born at the limits of viability should integrate clinical, neuroimaging and environmental risk factors to form a baseline prognostic opinion. This initial prognostic opinion can be refined as new findings emerge during the prolonged NICU admission. Major neonatal morbidities and everyday NICU exposures modify the developmental trajectory, while preterm brain injury adds more specificity to the possible range of outcomes. Counseling should avoid binary predictions, frame prognosis as a spectrum of probabilities, acknowledge uncertainty, and update expectations as the clinical course evolves. A structured counseling framework supports transparent risk communication, shared decision-making, and early developmental intervention.

## Efficacy of Lactobacillus reuteri DSM 17938 in preterm newborns and infants with colic: a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized controlled trials.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Nutrition · 中科院医学3区(营养学3区) · IF 3.6 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 意大利 · Sapienza University of Rome
- 通讯作者: Di Nardo G (giovanni.dinardo@icloud.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.nut.2026.113327
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.nut.2026.113327) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42485904/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估单一菌株罗伊氏乳杆菌DSM 17938在早产儿与婴儿肠绞痛中的疗效，通过系统综述和荟萃分析随机对照试验。
- 核心发现/数据：在肠绞痛婴儿中，益生菌组治疗反应率显著高于安慰剂组（OR 6.98; 95% CI 2.26-21.59），每日哭闹时间减少（SD -2.61; 95% CI -3.98至-1.24）。在早产儿中，益生菌降低了喂养不耐受发生率（OR 0.57; 95% CI 0.42-0.79）和坏死性小肠结肠炎发生率（OR 0.49; 95% CI 0.31-0.77），并缩短了达全肠内喂养时间和住院时间；对脓毒症无显著影响，未发现安全问题。
- 临床启示：罗伊氏乳杆菌DSM 17938可能有助于改善婴儿肠绞痛以及早产儿的特定胃肠道结局，但需更多高质量研究验证后方可常规应用于临床。

**原文摘要**：Early-life gut microbiota is crucial for gastrointestinal and immune development. Dysbiosis has been implicated in infantile colic and in complications of prematurity, including feeding intolerance and necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC). Lactobacillus reuteri DSM-17938 has been proposed as a strategy to modulate gut homeostasis during early infancy. We conducted a systematic review and meta-analysis of randomized, placebo-controlled trials evaluating single-strain L. reuteri DSM-17938 in newborns and infants (0-12 mo). PubMed, Scopus, Web of Science, and the Cochrane Library were searched from 2008 to October 2025. Two separate meta-analyses were conducted for colicky infants and preterm newborns. Primary outcomes were treatment response in colic (≥50% reduction in crying time) and feeding intolerance in preterms. Secondary outcomes for preterms included daily crying time, NEC, sepsis, time to full enteral feeding, and length of hospital stay. About 11 randomized, placebo-controlled trials involving infants with colic (n = 555), and eight involving preterms (n = 1704) were included. In colicky infants, L. reuteri DSM-17938 significantly increased treatment response compared with placebo (OR 6.98;95% CI 2.26-21.59) and significantly reduced daily crying time (SD -2.61;95% CI -3.98 to-1.24). In preterms, probiotic supplementation reduced the incidence of feeding intolerance (OR 0.57; 95% CI 0.42-0.79) and NEC (OR 0.49; 95% CI 0.31-0.77), with earlier achievement of full enteral feeding and shorter hospitalization. No significant effect on sepsis was observed, and no safety concerns emerged. L. reuteri DSM-17938 may be associated with benefits in infantile colic and selected gastrointestinal outcomes in preterm newborns. Further well-designed trials are needed to confirm these findings.

## Age-adjusted eosinophilia in preterm infants with feeding intolerance: a retrospective cohort study.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: BMC Gastroenterol · 中科院医学3区(胃肠肝病学4区) · IF 3.2 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: Kocaeli · Kocaeli University Faculty of Medicine
- 通讯作者: Serce Pehlevan O (ozge_serce@hotmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1186/s12876-026-05093-w
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1186/s12876-026-05093-w) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42481963/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探讨早产儿中年龄校正的外周血嗜酸性粒细胞增多与喂养不耐受（FI）的关联，并初步评估其与坏死性小肠结肠炎（NEC）及牛奶蛋白相关胃肠道疾病的关系。  
- 核心发现/数据：在122例嗜酸粒细胞计数≥700/mm³的早产儿中，100例存在年龄校正的嗜酸性粒细胞增多，其FI发生率显著高于非嗜酸组（86% vs. 63.6%, p=0.027），且喂养中断中位天数更长（2天 vs. 0.5天，p=0.028），腹胀更多（60% vs. 31.8%, p=0.019），住院时间延长（60天 vs. 41天，p=0.034）；多变量分析中年龄校正嗜酸增多与FI独立相关（OR 6.00, 95%CI 1.32-27.26, p=0.020），但敏感性分析后关联减弱（OR 2.87, 95%CI 0.86-9.58, p=0.087）；全部13例NEC均发生在嗜酸组，11例疑似牛奶蛋白相关疾病在饮食回避后改善。  
- 临床启示：早产儿中年龄校正的嗜酸性粒细胞增多常伴随更严重的FI表现，可能提示潜在的免疫/炎症反应，虽非直接因果，但在持续性FI或NEC样表现的鉴别中，嗜酸细胞计数可提供辅助诊断信息。

**原文摘要**：Feeding intolerance (FI) remains diagnostically challenging because of nonspecific symptoms and overlap with multiple gastrointestinal conditions. This study investigated the association between peripheral eosinophilia and FI in preterm infants with exploratory evaluation of necrotizing enterocolitis (NEC) and cow's milk protein-related gastrointestinal disease in relation to eosinophilia. In this retrospective cohort study, 122 preterm infants (< 34 weeks' gestation, < 2000 g) with eosinophil counts ≥ 700/mm³ were included. Infants were subsequently reclassified according to gestational- and postnatal-age-adjusted eosinophil reference values. Clinical features of FI, NEC, and cow's milk protein-related gastrointestinal disease were compared between infants with and without age-adjusted eosinophilia. Multivariable logistic regression was used to explore clinical variables independently associated with feeding intolerance. Age-adjusted eosinophilia was present in 100 infants. FI was more frequent in eosinophilic infants (86% vs. 63.6%, p = 0.027), with longer feeding interruption (median 2 vs. 0.5 days, p = 0.028), more abdominal distension (60% vs. 31.8%, p = 0.019), and prolonged hospitalization (60 vs. 41 days, p = 0.034). In the primary multivariable model, age-adjusted eosinophilia was independently associated with feeding intolerance (OR 6.00; 95% CI 1.32-27.26; p = 0.020), although the association was attenuated in sensitivity analysis (OR 2.87; 95% CI 0.86-9.58; p = 0.087). All 13 NEC cases occurred in eosinophilic infants, although the temporal relationship between eosinophilia and NEC varied. Suspected cow's milk protein-related gastrointestinal disease was identified in 11 infants, all of whom showed clinical improvement following dietary elimination. Age-adjusted eosinophilia was frequently observed in preterm infants with FI and more severe gastrointestinal manifestations. While unlikely to be causally related to FI, it may reflect an accompanying immune or inflammatory response during gastrointestinal disease. Eosinophil counts may provide supportive information in selected infants with persistent FI or NEC-like presentations, particularly when alternative inflammatory gastrointestinal processes are being considered.

## A randomized trial of an exclusive human milk diet in neonates with single-ventricle physiology: 3 and 6 months follow-up outcomes.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Pediatr Res · 中科院医学3区(儿科2区) · IF 3.3 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 美国 · University of Texas Health Science Center at San Antonio
- 通讯作者: Blanco CL (blanco@uthscsa.edu)
- DOI: 10.1038/s41390-026-05255-2
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41390-026-05255-2) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42481630/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估单心室生理新生儿接受纯母乳饮食后3-6个月的生长和健康结局。
- 核心发现/数据：76名婴儿完成出院评估，74名完成3-6个月随访，两组生长指标（z评分）和住院率无显著差异；出生至3-6个月期间中位生长速度分别为25克/天和22克/天；50-60%的婴儿在3-6月龄时实现100%经口喂养。
- 临床启示：单心室患儿可从标准化营养方案中获益，其生长速度可达正常水平；可考虑将胃造口置管延迟至Glenn手术后。

**原文摘要**：A prospective randomized controlled trial of exclusive human milk nutrition (EHM) in infants with single-ventricle physiology (SVP) was previously published. This study evaluates growth and health outcomes at 3-6 months. Demographics, anthropometrics, and clinical outcomes were collected prospectively. Anthropometric z-scores were calculated using WHO standards. One hundred seven infants were randomized to EHM or control diet, with 23 infants meeting predefined exclusion criteria, 8 infants withdrawn/died, leaving 76 infants at discharge. At 3-6 months, 74 infants completed follow-up. Infants with hypoplastic left heart syndrome (HLHS) were similar (control 73%, EHM 86.5%; p = 0.24). Growth, z-scores, and hospitalization did not differ between groups up to 6 months. Median growth velocity was 25 (IQR: 20.24-30.99) and 22 (IQR: 18.76-23.94) g/day from birth to 3-6 months. Overall, 50-60% of infants achieved 100% PO intake by 3-6 months of age. Neonates with SVP receiving an EHM diet from birth to 30 days post-surgery had similar growth at 3-6 months of age compared to the control diet. Infants with SVP benefit from a protocolized nutritional approach, as evidenced by normal growth velocities in infancy. Centers may consider delaying gastrostomy tube placement until after the Glenn operation. This trial is registered with ClinicalTrials.gov ( www. gov , Trial ID: NCT02860702). This trial was approved by the Institutional Review Board of the University of Texas Health Sciences Center at San Antonio (ID: HSC20150779H). This multicenter RCT demonstrates the long-term benefits of exclusive human milk nutrition in infants with SVP, with improved growth and reduced necrotizing enterocolitis. A multidisciplinary approach and a well-thought-out nutritional algorithm can optimize growth in infants with complex congenital heart disease, similar to healthy term infants up to 6 months of age. The majority of infants with SVP requiring an operation within the first month of life are able to achieve 100% of their intake via PO by 6 months of age; therefore, delaying gastrostomy tube placement until after the Glenn operation may be considered.

## Neighborhood-Level Socioeconomic Disadvantage and Adverse Outcomes for Infants with Biliary Atresia in the United States.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区) · IF 3.6 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: San Francisco · University of California San Francisco
- 通讯作者: Highet A (alexandra.highet@ucsf.edu)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpeds.2026.115252
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpeds.2026.115252) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42492658/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探讨美国胆道闭锁婴儿的社区社会经济劣势水平（儿童机会指数，COI）与Kasai肝门肠吻合术（HPE）手术时机及术后医疗利用之间的关联。
- 核心发现/数据：在1182名婴儿中，最不利社区（很低COI）的婴儿接受HPE的年龄比很高COI的婴儿晚8天（β=8.00，95% CI 2.00-11.00，p=0.001）；COI与术后住院时间无关；很低COI与全因再入院风险增加相关（单变量HR=1.38，95% CI 1.14-1.68，p=0.001），但在调整种族、民族及保险状态后失去统计学意义（HR=1.22，95% CI 0.98-1.51，p=0.076）。
- 临床启示：基于社区社会经济水平的针对性干预（如利用COI识别高风险人群）有助于改善胆道闭锁婴儿及时获得外科手术的机会。

**原文摘要**：To examine the associations between child opportunity index (COI) and time to surgery as well as postoperative healthcare utilization for infants with biliary atresia undergoing Kasai hepatoportoenterostomy (HPE) in the USA. We conducted a retrospective cohort study of all infants with biliary atresia who underwent HPE within the Pediatric Health Information System database between 2014-2024. Our primary exposure was the COI, a composite measure of socioeconomic status at the ZIP code level. Primary outcomes were age at HPE in days; post-HPE length of stay (LOS); and hospital revisits (inpatient, observation, and emergency department) within two years post-HPE. We modeled median age at HPE using quantile regression, postoperative LOS using negative binomial regression, and revisits using Anderson-Gill recurrent event models with robust standard errors. We compared unadjusted models, models adjusted for race and ethnicity, and models adjusted for race and ethnicity as well as insurance status. Among 1,182 infants at 48 hospitals, median COI was 43 (Q1-Q3 20, 69) and median age at HPE was 56 days (Q1-Q3 38, 70). After adjustment, infants in the 20% most disadvantaged neighborhoods (very low COI) underwent HPE 8 days later than very high COI infants (β = 8.00, 95% CI 2.00-11.00, p=0.001). COI was not associated with postoperative LOS. Very low COI was associated with higher risk of all-cause readmission in the univariable model (HR 1.38, 95% CI 1.14-1.68, p=0.001) but lost significance in the model adjusted for race and ethnicity as well as insurance status (HR 1.22, 0.98-1.51, p=0.076). Although infants with biliary atresia from the most socioeconomically disadvantaged neighborhoods undergo HPE at later ages, neighborhood-level socioeconomic status was not associated with postoperative LOS or readmissions. Targeted interventions using the COI could improve timely access to surgical care for this vulnerable population.

## Perioperative multidisciplinary nursing care for a pediatric patient with biliary atresia and Alagille syndrome undergoing liver transplantation: A case report.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: J Pediatr Nurs
- 国家/单位: 中国 · Tianjin First Central Hospital
- 通讯作者: Zhang Z (260697216@qq.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.pedn.2026.07.036
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.pedn.2026.07.036) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42492415/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报告一例胆道闭锁合并Alagille综合征的5岁患儿接受肝移植的围手术期多学科护理经验。
- 核心发现/数据：患儿住院第2天行原位肝移植，术后出现乳糜性腹水和胆道吻合口狭窄，总住院78天；术后3个月远程随访移植物功能稳定，PTCD导管于术后1个月暂时夹闭，照护者焦虑显著缓解。
- 临床启示：复杂小儿肝移植护理应采用多学科团队指导的分步营养方案、标准化症状评估工具及数字化远程医疗赋权模式，以保障安全有效的家庭护理过渡。

**原文摘要**：This case report details the perioperative management of a 5-year-old girl with biliary atresia (BA) and Alagille syndrome (ALGS) undergoing liver transplantation. Our transplant center emphasizes preoperative optimization, targeted management of high-risk complications, and family-centered support throughout the care process. This report was prepared in strict accordance with the Case Report (CARE) Guidelines, an official clinical reporting guideline endorsed by the EQUATOR Network, to ensure standardized and transparent case reporting. This case report details the perioperative management of a 5-year-old girl with BA and ALGS undergoing liver transplantation. The clinical strategy highlights preoperative optimization, targeted intervention for high-risk complications, and family-centered care throughout the treatment course. The patient underwent orthotopic liver transplantation on the second hospital day. Postoperative complications included chylous ascites and biliary anastomotic stricture. The total length of hospital stay was 78 days. During 3 months of remote follow-up, her graft function remained stable. The percutaneous transhepatic cholangiography (PTCD) catheter was temporarily clamped at 1 month postoperatively, and anxiety in the patient's caregiver was significantly alleviated. This case report presents a novel integrated strategy encompassing multidisciplinary team evaluation, stepwise nutritional optimization, and remote digital follow-up.This approach effectively supported postoperative recovery in this case study, consistently maintained the function of the allograft, and facilitated a successful transition to home care. Nursing care for complex pediatric liver transplantation should incorporate a multidisciplinary team (MDT)-led, stepwise nutritional protocol for the management of lymphatic complications, standardized symptom assessment instruments, and a patient empowerment model integrated with digital telemedicine to support safe and effective transition to home care.

## Preoperative porta hepatis-to-hepatic surface ratio on 24-h delayed hepatobiliary scintigraphy is associated with jaundice clearance after Kasai portoenterostomy.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Pediatr Surg Int · 中科院医学3区(儿科3区/外科3区) · IF 1.8 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 日本 · Saitama Children's Medical Center
- 通讯作者: Deie K (pasers2006@gmail.com)
- DOI: 10.1007/s00383-026-06550-z
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00383-026-06550-z) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42474526/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估术前24小时延迟肝胆闪烁显像中肝门部-肝脏表面（PH/HS）比值对Kasai肝门肠吻合术后黄疸清除的预测价值。
- 核心发现/数据：实现黄疸清除的16例婴儿中位PH/HS比值更高（1.20 vs. 1.11，p=0.049）；ROC分析显示探索性截断值1.15（表观AUC 0.768，内部验证AUC 0.670）；排除1年内移植患儿后，PH/HS比值与1年胆红素水平呈负相关（r=-0.720）。
- 临床启示：PH/HS比值作为一种无创术前参数，可辅助预测Kasai术后黄疸清除，但结果仍为初步假设生成性，需前瞻性多中心验证。

**原文摘要**：Biliary atresia (BA) often requires liver transplantation despite Kasai portoenterostomy (KPE). The prognostic role of hepatobiliary scintigraphy remains unclear. Twenty-three infants undergoing KPE between 2017 and 2024 were retrospectively analyzed. Patients without 6-h bowel activity on technetium-99 m PMT hepatobiliary scintigraphy underwent 24-h delayed imaging. The primary endpoint was jaundice clearance (total bilirubin < 2 mg/dL within 3 months). Ancillary analyses included an internally validated receiver operating characteristic (ROC) cutoff and correlation of the 24-h porta hepatis-to-hepatic surface (PH/HS) ratio with 1-year bilirubin, native liver survival (NLS), and portal plate bile duct diameter. Sixteen infants achieved jaundice clearance, and their 24-h PH/HS ratio was higher (1.20 vs. 1.11, p = 0.049). ROC analysis identified an exploratory cutoff of 1.15 (apparent area under the curve [AUC], 0.768; internally validated AUC, 0.670). Excluding patients transplanted within 1 year, the PH/HS ratio correlated inversely with 1-year bilirubin (r =  - 0.720). NLS did not differ significantly between groups, and the association with portal plate bile duct diameter was weak. The 24-h PH/HS ratio was associated with post-KPE jaundice clearance. However, these findings remain preliminary and hypothesis-generating. As a noninvasive preoperative parameter beyond routine laboratory testing, the PH/HS ratio warrants prospective multicenter validation.

## Population-Based Analysis of Prevalence, Associated Anomalies, and Mortality in Anorectal Malformations in Finland (2004-2017).

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Eur J Pediatr Surg
- 国家/单位: Turku · University of Turku
- 通讯作者: Raitio A
- DOI: 10.1055/a-2915-1984
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1055/a-2915-1984) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42457155/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：调查芬兰人群中肛门直肠畸形（ARM）的患病率、相关异常及死亡率，并基于最新人群数据更新死产和因胎儿异常终止妊娠（TOPFA）的统计。  
- 核心发现/数据：2004-2017年间共识别646例ARM，总患病率为8.00/万出生，其中活产545例（84.4%），死产8例（1.2%），TOPFA 93例（14.4%）；患病率在研究期间保持稳定；新生儿死亡率为4.6%，伴严重相关异常的患儿死亡率显著更高。  
- 临床启示：ARM患儿需接受系统性筛查以发现伴随畸形，尤其应关注伴有严重相关异常的高危新生儿，以改善预后管理并降低死亡率。

**原文摘要**：Anorectal malformations (ARMs) represent the most common form of congenital gastrointestinal malformations. ARMs range from mild anal stenosis to complex abnormalities involving the rectum, genitals, and urinary tract. The aim of this study is to investigate prevalence, mortality, and associated anomalies of ARMs in the Finnish population. This study offers comprehensive updated population-based estimates on stillbirths and terminations of pregnancy due to fetal anomalies (TOPFA). The data were obtained on all live births, TOPFA, and stillbirths from registers maintained by the Finnish Institute for Health and Welfare and Statistics Finland. All individuals diagnosed with ARM between January 1, 2004 and December 31, 2017, were included in the study. This population-based study identified 646 ARM cases, including 545 (84.4%) live births, 8 (1.2%) stillbirths, and 93 (14.4%) TOPFA. The total prevalence for ARM in Finland was 8.00 per 10,000 births, without significant variation in prevalence between 2004 and 2017. Neonatal mortality was 4.6% ( The overall prevalence of ARMs has remained stable in the Finnish population. Neonatal and infant mortality remains a significant concern, particularly among patients with severe associated anomalies, highlighting the importance of systematic screening for co-occurring malformations.

## PHOX2B polyalanine repeat mutation alters the transcriptome of neuronal progenitor cells in congenital central hypoventilation syndrome.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Neurobiol Dis · 中科院医学2区(神经科学2区) · IF 6.0 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 美国 · School of Medicine
- 通讯作者: Stobdan T (tstobdan@health.ucsd.edu)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.nbd.2026.107544
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.nbd.2026.107544) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42480943/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探究PHOX2B多聚丙氨酸重复突变（PARM）对先天性中枢性低通气综合征（CCHS）患者神经祖细胞转录组的影响。
- 核心发现/数据：来自CCHS/HS患者的神经上皮干细胞（NESCs）中，单拷贝PHOX2B-PARM导致超过8倍的差异表达基因，包括神经发育相关基因STMN2、L1CAM、ONECUT2和NFASC；神经元发育和突触组织相关基因（主要涉及L1CAM相互作用和突触形成信号通路）显著富集。
- 临床启示：该研究为CCHS/HS的神经发育表型提供了合适的细胞模型，并明确了未来实验验证的关键方向，有助于深入理解疾病机制。

**原文摘要**：Mutation in paired-like homeobox 2B (PHOX2B) is used as the diagnostic marker of Congenital Central Hypoventilation Syndrome (CCHS). The mutant gene/protein affects neural crest cells embryonic development which leads to congenital central hypoventilation syndrome (CCHS). When individuals also have Hirschsprung's disease (HSCR) with CCHS it is known as Haddad syndrome (HS). Previous studies on CCHS/HS have mainly focused on the conformational dynamics of the mutant protein and have remained controversial. Here we performed RNA-sequencing on the patient derived neuroepithelial stem cells (NESCs), pertinent to the neurodevelopmental phenotype in CCHS/HS, and found that the PHOX2B-PARM has a profound impact on the transcriptional profile of the cells. The single copy of PHOX2B-PARM in heterozygote cells led to >8 fold differentially expressed genes. This include genes e.g., STMN2, L1CAM, ONECUT2 and NFASC, that are reported to have role in neurodevelopment. In the patient cells there was a significant enrichment of genes related to neuronal development and synapse organization mainly driven by L1CAM interactions and synaptogenesis signaling pathway. Our results not only highlight the use of a suitable model of CCHS/HS but also provide a clear path for future experimental validation.

## Impact of a low-cost lamb anus Hirschsprung pull-through surgery model: results from a structured national workshop.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Pediatr Surg Int · 中科院医学3区(儿科3区/外科3区) · IF 1.8 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 马来西亚 · Universiti Malaya and University of Malaya Medical Centre
- 通讯作者: Tan YW (ywtan80@doctors.net.uk)
- DOI: 10.1007/s00383-026-06523-2
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00383-026-06523-2) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42474777/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估使用低成本羔羊肛门模型进行全国性赫希施普龙病Soave拖出术模拟培训的效果，包括手术信心、技能获取及临床转化。
- 核心发现/数据：17名参与者在14名导师指导下完成34次模拟手术，总体手术信心中位数从2.5提升至7.0（P<0.0005），其中粘膜下剥离、袖套管理和吻合步骤信心提升最大（均从2.0或3.0升至8.0，P<0.001）。模型真实感和培训实用性评分均为9/10（93%应答率），3-6个月随访中100%的受访者认为培训提升了辅助或实施该手术的能力。
- 临床启示：该低成本（<5美元）、高保真度的模型可规模化推广，有效提升外科医生手术信心并转化为临床实践中的行为改变，为新生儿外科培训提供了伦理且高效的解决方案。

**原文摘要**：A nationwide simulated Hirschsprung's Soave pull-through surgery (HPTS) workshop using a lamb model was set up to train pediatric surgeons. Operative confidence, skills acquisition and clinical application were evaluated. The model (< 5 USD) comprised fresh lamb anorectum with terminal perianal muscle and skin preserved and a 20-cm rectum, inserted and fixed onto a mini basket, which allowed dissection using monopolar diathermy. Each trainee performed the five-step Soave procedure twice under supervision. Operative confidence for each step was measured pre- and post-workshop using a 10-point Likert scale, with model realism and utility similarly rated. A 3-6-month follow-up survey assessed clinical translation. Seventeen participants and 14 trainers from nine centres facilitated completion of 34 simulated procedures. Median overall confidence improved from 2.5 to 7.0 (P < 0.0005), with the greatest gains obtained in submucosal dissection (2.0 to 8.0), cuff management (2.0 to 8.0) and anastomosis (3.0 to 8.0) (all P < 0.001). Model realism and training utility were rated 9/10 and 9/10, respectively, by 25 respondents (93% response rate). Follow-up survey (12/17; 71%) showed 100% agreement that the workshop improved ability to assist or perform HPTS. This structured simulation-based HPTS workshop, using a validated low-cost model, significantly improved procedural confidence which translated into behavioural change in clinical practice. The model offers high fidelity, scalability and ethical training.

## Formative perception of 4th and 5th year Pediatric Surgery residents in Spain.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Cir Pediatr
- 国家/单位: Navarra (Spain) · Pediatric Surgery Department. Hospital Universitario de Navarra. Pamplona
- 通讯作者: Pérez Martínez A
- DOI: 10.54847/cp.2026.03.14
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.54847/cp.2026.03.14) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42487422/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估西班牙第四年和第五年儿科外科住院医师对自主性的感知及培训的优劣势，以识别手术和临床培训中需改进的领域。
- 核心发现/数据：共18名住院医师参与（平均年龄29.6岁，61%为R5，39%为R4），R4在常见手术（如开放睾丸固定术、腹股沟疝修补术）中自主性较高，在新生儿手术和泌尿外科病理中自主性较低；R5在常规普通外科手术中独立性更强，在食管闭锁、膈疝等复杂手术中自主性较低。主要限制包括新生儿手术量少、住院医师数量多及结构化培训活动不足。
- 临床启示：需在全国范围内协调培训策略，如引入手术模拟、结构化在线培训及转诊至参考中心轮转，以优化能力获取并确保培训尽可能完善。

**原文摘要**：To evaluate the perception of autonomy and the main formative strengths and weaknesses of fourth-year (R4) and fifth-year (R5) Pediatric Surgery residents (MIR) in Spain, in order to identify areas for improvement in their surgical and clinical training. A descriptive cross-sectional study was conducted using an anonymous survey distributed via Google Forms during the last quarter of 2023. The questionnaire included 8 questions on demographic data, surgical activity, outpatient clinics, and training suggestions. The results underwent a descriptive statistical analysis. Eighteen residents participated (11 women and 7 men), with a mean age of 29.6 years; 61% were MIR-5 and 39% MIR-4. R4s perceived greater autonomy in frequent procedures (open orchidopexy, inguinal herniorrhaphy, appendectomy, and upper gastrointestinal endoscopy) and less in neonatal surgery and urological pathology. R5s reported greater independence in common general surgery and less in esophageal atresia, diaphragmatic hernia, pyeloplasty, and nephrectomy. In outpatient clinics, autonomy was greater in abdominal and skin pathology, and lower in urology and head and neck pathology. Among the main limitations noted were the low volume of neonatal surgery, the high number of residents, and the scarce offer of structured training activities. The lower perceived autonomy is concentrated in neonatal surgery and complex urological pathology. Coordinated training strategies are required at the national level, including surgical simulation, structured online training, and rotations in reference centers, to optimize the acquisition of competencies and ensure that training is as complete as possible. Evaluar la percepción de autonomía y las principales fortalezas y debilidades formativas de los médicos internos residentes (MIR) de 4º (R4) y 5º año (R5) de cirugía pediátrica en España, con el fin de identificar áreas de mejora en su formación quirúrgica y asistencial. Estudio descriptivo transversal mediante encuesta anónima distribuida por Google Forms durante el último trimestre de 2023. El cuestionario incluyó 8 preguntas sobre datos demográficos, actividad quirúrgica, consultas externas y sugerencias formativas. Los resultados fueron sometidos a un análisis estadístico descriptivo. Participaron 18 residentes (11 mujeres y 7 varones), con una edad media de 29,6 años; 61% eran MIR-5 y 39% MIR-4. Los R4 percibían mayor autonomía en procedimientos frecuentes (orquidopexia abierta, herniorrafia inguinal, apendicectomía y endoscopia digestiva alta) y menor en cirugía neonatal y patología urológica. Los R5 referían mayor independencia en cirugía general habitual y menor en atresia esofágica, hernia diafragmática, pieloplastia y nefrectomía. En consultas externas, la autonomía fue mayor en patología abdominal y cutánea, y menor en urología y patología de cabeza y cuello. Entre las principales limitaciones señaladas destacaron el bajo volumen de cirugía neonatal, el elevado número de residentes y la escasa oferta de actividades formativas estructuradas. La menor autonomía percibida se concentra en cirugía neonatal y patología urológica compleja. Se requieren estrategias formativas coordinadas a nivel nacional, incluyendo simulación quirúrgica, formación online estructurada y rotaciones en centros de referencia, para optimizar la adquisición de competencias y garantizar que la formación sea lo más completa posible.

## Feeding jejunostomy in neonates with complicated esophageal atresia: A retrospective descriptive single-center study.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Nutr Clin Pract
- 国家/单位: 土耳其 · Başkent University Faculty of Medicine
- 通讯作者: Temiz A
- DOI: 10.1002/ncp.70160
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1002/ncp.70160) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42482379/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：评估在复杂食管闭锁（包括吻合口漏）新生儿中行喂养性空肠造口术（FJ）的可行性和安全性。
- 核心发现/数据：在41例EA修复中，吻合口漏发生率为17.1%（7例），共实施10例FJ（3例预防性），技术均成功且无术中或重大术后并发症。FJ置管后中位3天（范围2-4天）实现全肠内营养，6例AL患者自FJ置管至影像学漏口闭合的中位时间为9天（范围6-14天）；无FJ相关严重并发症，无新发胆汁淤积或CLABSI，3例转诊患者（中位PN 30天）原有胆汁淤积和/或CLABSI在FJ后均改善。
- 临床启示：FJ在复杂EA（包括合并AL）新生儿中可行且安全，能快速实现全肠内营养并减少PN相关并发症，为高危人群的管理提供了有价值的初步证据，有待前瞻性多中心研究验证。

**原文摘要**：Anastomotic leak (AL) complicates 8%-29% of esophageal atresia (EA) repairs, precluding safe trans-anastomotic enteral feeding. Parenteral nutrition (PN) carries additional risks in neonates. Feeding jejunostomy (FJ) provides an alternative enteral route that bypasses the anastomosis, but data remain limited. This study describes the feasibility and safety outcomes of FJ in this clinical setting. Single-center retrospective cohort study (2018-2023) of neonates undergoing EA/tracheoesophageal fistula (TEF) repair or transferred to this hospital for AL after EA repair. Among 41 repairs, 7 AL occurred (17.1%), including 3 transfers with pre-existing AL after prolonged PN. Ten FJ procedures were performed (3 prophylactically) and were technically successful with no intraoperative or major postoperative complications. One patient (10%) had a minor FJ catheter occlusion managed by bedside catheter exchange without interruption of enteral nutrition. No major FJ-related complications occurred. Among 6 FJ-managed AL patients, the median time from FJ placement to radiologic leak closure was 9 days (range 6-14). Full enteral nutrition was achieved at a median of 3 days (range 2-4) in all FJ patients. No FJ patient developed new-onset cholestasis or central line-associated bloodstream infection (CLABSI). The 3 transfers (median PN 30 days; range 28-33) had pre-existing cholestasis and/or CLABSI at presentation; all improved after FJ placement and enteral feeding. In this descriptive cohort, FJ was feasible and safe, enabling rapid full enteral nutrition in neonates with complicated EA, including those with AL. Despite the small sample size, these hypothesis-generating observations warrant prospective multicenter investigation in this high-risk population.

## Engineering the esophagus: advances, challenges, and translational pathways in esophageal tissue reconstruction.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Biofabrication · 中科院医学2区(工程：生物医学2区/材料科学：生物材料2区) · IF 8.2 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 加拿大 · Esophageal Development and Engineering Laboratory
- 通讯作者: Faure C
- DOI: 10.1088/1758-5090/ae80ff
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1088/1758-5090/ae80ff) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42335933/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：综述食管组织工程（ETE）在支架材料、制造技术、细胞策略及临床转化方面的最新进展与挑战。
- 核心发现/数据：涵盖天然、合成及混合生物材料在理想支架中的应用，以及原代细胞、干细胞和类器官等细胞方法；在啮齿类、兔、犬和猪等动物模型中观察到功能恢复与再生能力，但临床转化仍面临监管、GMP合规及长期监测等障碍。
- 临床启示：ETE有望克服传统食管重建术（如胃上提、肠间置）导致的吞咽困难、狭窄和反流等长期并发症，为患者提供更符合生理的再生治疗方案，但需通过更多临床试验验证其安全性与有效性。

**原文摘要**：Esophageal reconstruction is one of the most challenging procedures in gastrointestinal surgery. While conventional therapeutic approaches, such as gastric pull-up and intestinal interposition, can restore continuity, they often fail to replicate native physiology. This limitation frequently leads to long-term complications, including dysphagia, stricture, and reflux, which can significantly affect the patients' quality of life. Tissue engineering approaches offer promising alternatives aimed at developing esophageal constructs that restore both structure and function, addressing the shortcomings of current treatment methods. This review highlights recent progress in esophageal tissue engineering (ETE), focusing on the requirements for ideal ETE scaffolds and examining available biomaterials, including natural, synthetic, and hybrid. We discuss advances in fabrication techniques and various cell-based approaches, such as primary cells, stem cells, and organoids. Furthermore, we also review the steps necessary to transition ETE constructs from the laboratory to clinical settings (ongoing human trials), including preclinical studies conducted on rodent, rabbit, canine, and porcine models with the expected functional outcomes and regeneration capabilities. Early translational efforts in ETE are addressed, along with the regulatory and ethical considerations regarding good manufacturing practice (GMP) compliance, traceability, and long-term surveillance. While significant advancements in ETE have been made in preclinical models, the review also discusses the challenges of moving to clinical studies. Potential strategies to address these challenges, such as 4-dimensional printing, smart materials, artificial intelligence-driven scaffold optimization, and organoid-based models, are introduced to help bridge the gap from preclinical research to successful clinical trials. In summary, ETE is transitioning from an experimental advancement to a translational reality by integrating significant achievements in biomaterials, fabrication technologies, and cell biology while following health regulatory standards. These efforts aim to provide regenerative solutions that overcome the limitations of current therapeutic approaches in clinical settings, ultimately facilitating healing and improving the patients' quality of life.

## Sacrococcygeal teratoma associated with complex gastroschisis with favorable outcome. A case report.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Cir Pediatr
- 国家/单位: Uruguay · Assistant. Clínica Quirúrgica Pediátrica Prof. Dr. Gabriel Giannini. School of Medicine
- 通讯作者: Giannini G
- DOI: 10.54847/cp.2026.03.18
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.54847/cp.2026.03.18) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42487426/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报道一例罕见的骶尾部畸胎瘤合并复杂性腹裂（伴肠穿孔）新生儿，通过分阶段手术获得良好预后的治疗经验。
- 核心发现/数据：全球仅1例类似病例报道，本例为首例存活且预后良好的患儿。治疗分4个阶段（腹裂关闭+肠造口、畸胎瘤切除、使用Santulli术式纠正闭锁、关闭造口），总耗时6个月18天，患儿术后生长发育良好。
- 临床启示：针对罕见复合畸形，分阶段手术规划可逐一处理各病理问题，降低并发症风险，为患儿生长提供时间窗口。

**原文摘要**：We present the case of a newborn with sacrococcygeal teratoma and complex gastroschisis, complicated with intestinal perforation. This is an unusual association, with only 1 reported case worldwide, and this is the first reported case that survived with a good outcome. The treatment was conducted in 4 stages: gastroschisis closure and enterostomy delaying the treatment of the atresia, teratoma resection, correction of the atresia using the Santulli technique and closure of the stoma. It took 6 months and 18 days to complete the whole treatment. The patient showed excellent progress with good growth and subsequent development. The treatment conducted in 4 stages made possible the resolution of each pathology, with proper assessment and surgical planning, making possible the patient's development and growth while minimizing the risks of complications. Presentamos el caso de una recién nacida con teratoma sacrococcígeo y gastrosquisis compleja, complicada con perforación intestinal. Se trata de una asociación inusual, habiendo 1 caso reportado a nivel mundial y siendo este el primero reportado que sobrevive, con buena evolución. Se realizó el tratamiento en 4 etapas: tratamiento de la gastrosquisis con incubación de las asas y enterostomía; resección del teratoma; reconstrucción del tránsito con ileostomía funcionalizante y cierre de la ostomía. Requirió un total de 6 meses y 18 días. Presentó excelente evolución con buen crecimiento y desarrollo posterior. El tratamiento realizado en 4 etapas permitió la resolución oportuna de cada una de las patologías, con la correcta valoración y planificación quirúrgica, permitiendo el desarrollo y crecimiento de la paciente a la vez que se redujeron al mínimo los riesgos de complicaciones.

---

## Fetal lung interstitial tumor (FLIT): a case report and review of prenatal imaging features.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Pediatr Radiol · 中科院医学3区(儿科2区/核医学3区) · IF 2.5 · JCR Q1
- 国家/单位: 美国 · Creighton University
- 通讯作者: Hoang AT (aho52703@creighton.edu)
- DOI: 10.1007/s00247-026-06732-w
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00247-026-06732-w) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42496696/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报告一例产前发现的胎儿肺间质肿瘤（FLIT）并回顾其产前影像学特征。  
- 核心发现/数据：FLIT是一种罕见的良性间叶性肺肿瘤，产前常被误诊为先天性肺气道畸形（CPAM）；本例在妊娠晚期快速增大，导致纵隔移位和胎儿水肿（胸腔积液、腹水、皮肤水肿），产前超声和MRI显示右肺肿块；出生后行肺叶切除术，组织病理学证实为未成熟间叶组织增生，符合FLIT诊断。  
- 临床启示：FLIT应与CPAM及胸膜肺母细胞瘤鉴别，产前影像学有重叠，确诊需组织病理学，完整手术切除预后良好。

**原文摘要**：Fetal lung interstitial tumor (FLIT) is a rare benign mesenchymal lung tumor that is often mistaken for more common congenital lung lesions such as congenital pulmonary airway malformation (CPAM). Prenatal detection is exceedingly uncommon. Here, we report a case of prenatally detected FLIT presenting in the third trimester with rapid interval growth, mediastinal shift, and fetal hydrops, raising concern for a malignancy, including pleuropulmonary blastoma. Prenatal ultrasonography and fetal magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) demonstrated a rapidly enlarging right lung mass first identified late in gestation with associated findings of pleural effusion, ascites, and diffuse skin edema consistent with hydrops. Progressive mass effect prompted expedited delivery. Postnatal imaging confirmed a large pulmonary lesion, and the neonate developed significant respiratory distress requiring lobectomy. Histopathologic evaluation revealed an immature mesenchymal proliferation diagnostic of FLIT. This case highlights the imaging overlap between FLIT, CPAM, and pleuropulmonary blastoma and underscores the importance of considering FLIT in the differential diagnosis of late-presenting fetal lung masses. Although imaging is essential for detection and prenatal planning, definitive diagnosis requires histopathologic confirmation, and complete surgical resection yields an excellent prognosis.

## Reduced KLF4 expression in endothelial cells triggers pulmonary vascular abnormalities associated with experimental congenital diaphragmatic hernia.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Cell Mol Life Sci
- 国家/单位: 荷兰 · Erasmus MC - Sophia Children's Hospital
- 通讯作者: Rottier RJ (r.rottier@erasmusmc.nl)
- DOI: 10.1007/s00018-026-06319-6
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1007/s00018-026-06319-6) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42474740/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探究先天性膈疝（CDH）中肺血管异常的早期分子机制，特别是内皮细胞中KLF4表达降低的作用。
- 核心发现/数据：在硝基芬诱导的CDH小鼠模型中，肺内皮细胞与血管周细胞的差异表达基因GO术语高度相似，提示两者相互作用紊乱；RNA测序及蛋白表达分析证实CDH小鼠内皮细胞中KLF4表达降低，且NOTCH信号通路成员表达异常，表明该通路在CDH中受到干扰。
- 临床启示：CDH患者的肺血管在出生前已存在异常，KLF4可能成为减轻CDH相关肺动脉高压的潜在治疗靶点。

**原文摘要**：Congenital diaphragmatic hernia (CDH) is a rare anomaly with an incidence of 1 in 3000 live births and characterized by defective closure of the diaphragm, lung hypoplasia, and pulmonary vascular remodeling. Although several genes associated with the occurrence of CDH, no clear genetic component has been identified. Previously, we showed that CDH patients have vascular abnormalities already early in development, and that perivascular cells are affected in CDH. In the current study, we focused on early abnormalities in the nitrofen-induced CDH mouse model. Transcriptome analysis of FACS-sorted perivascular and endothelial cell populations from lungs of control and experimental mouse CDH revealed a high similarity in GO terms of differentially expressed genes (DEGs) between these populations, suggesting a disturbed interaction between endothelial cells and pericytes. The disturbed interaction is the basis for the aberrant development of the pulmonary vasculature in CDH. Furthermore, the RNA sequence data revealed reduced expression of the Kruppel like factor 4 (Klf4) gene in endothelial cells of CDH, which was confirmed with protein expression analysis. Furthermore, we show that KLF4 is an important up stream regulator of genes associated with vascular development and confirm that members of the NOTCH signaling pathway are differentially expressed, indicating that NOTCH signaling is disturbed in CDH. Collectively, our data support the importance of KLF4 in pulmonary angiogenesis and contribute to our previous data that pulmonary vessels in CDH patients are already affected before birth, which in turn may impact therapeutic strategies to reduce pulmonary hypertension associated with CDH.

## Single-port transumbilical laparoscopic appendectomy combined with Ladd procedure in intestinal malrotation: A case report.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Cir Pediatr
- 国家/单位: 美国 · Pediatric Surgery Department. Hospital Universitari Quirón-Dexeus
- 通讯作者: Abello C
- DOI: 10.54847/cp.2026.03.20
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.54847/cp.2026.03.20) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42487428/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报告首例通过单孔经脐腹腔镜同时完成阑尾切除术与Ladd手术治疗肠旋转不良的病例，评估其可行性与安全性。
- 核心发现/数据：一名9岁女孩因急性阑尾炎（位于膀胱上方异常位置）合并肠旋转不良（无扭转），采用单孔经脐腹腔镜入路同时完成阑尾切除和Ladd手术，术后第1天出院；随访1年无症状，美容效果极佳。
- 临床启示：单孔经脐腹腔镜可安全、有效地同时处理阑尾炎和肠旋转不良，为特定儿科患者提供了一种微创替代方案。

**原文摘要**：Acute appendicitis associated with intestinal malrotation is uncommon and may pose diagnostic and technical challenges. We report the first described case of appendectomy associated with Ladd procedure performed through a single-port transumbilical laparoscopic approach. A 9-year-old girl presented with acute abdominal symptoms on a background of chronic recurrent abdominal pain. Imaging revealed acute appendicitis in an atypical supravesical position and inversion of mesenteric vessels consistent with intestinal malrotation without volvulus. A single-port transumbilical laparoscopic approach was used, allowing simultaneous appendectomy and Ladd procedure through the same umbilical access. The postoperative course was uneventful, and the patient was discharged on postoperative day one. At one year of follow-up, she remains asymptomatic with an excellent cosmetic result. Single-port transumbilical laparoscopy enabled safe and effective combined management of appendicitis and intestinal malrotation and may represent a minimally invasive alternative in selected pediatric patients. La apendicitis aguda en el contexto de malrotación intestinal supone un desafío diagnóstico y terapéutico. Presentamos el primer caso documentado de apendicectomía combinada con procedimiento de Ladd mediante laparoscopia por puerto único transumbilical. Niña de 9 años que consultó por clínica abdominal aguda sobre un antecedente de dolor abdominal recurrente crónico. Las pruebas de imagen mostraron apendicitis aguda en localización supravesical atípica e inversión de los vasos mesentéricos, compatible con malrotación intestinal sin vólvulo. Se realizó un abordaje laparoscópico transumbilical por puerto único, que permitió la realización simultánea de apendicectomía y procedimiento de Ladd a través del mismo acceso umbilical. La evolución postoperatoria fue favorable, con alta hospitalaria al primer día. Al año de seguimiento, la paciente permanece asintomática con excelente resultado cosmético. La laparoscopia transumbilical por puerto único permitió el tratamiento combinado seguro y eficaz de la apendicitis y la malrotación intestinal, y puede representar una alternativa mínimamente invasiva en pacientes pediátricos seleccionados.

## Challenging anatomy: situs inversus, duodenal web, and intestinal malrotation in a neonate.

- 来源: PubMed · 推送 09:06
- 期刊: Cir Pediatr
- 国家/单位: 阿根廷 · Pediatric Surgery Department. Hospital Italiano de Buenos Aires. Buenos Aires
- 通讯作者: Ges H
- DOI: 10.54847/cp.2026.03.19
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.54847/cp.2026.03.19) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42487427/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：报告一例罕见的新生儿全内脏转位合并肠旋转不良及十二指肠隔膜的病例，并强调其诊断与治疗策略。
- 核心发现/数据：全内脏转位全球发病率约为1/10,000活产儿；该足月新生儿出生第一天即表现为肠梗阻，胸腹影像及超声确诊全内脏转位和十二指肠梗阻，术中造影证实肠旋转不良；经Ladd手术及十二指肠隔膜切除后恢复良好。
- 临床启示：对伴有肠梗阻的全内脏转位新生儿，应考虑肠旋转不良和十二指肠隔膜作为可能并存诊断，影像学检查对制定手术策略至关重要。

**原文摘要**：Situs inversus totalis is a rare congenital anomaly, with an estimated incidence of 1 in 10,000 live births worldwide. Its association with intestinal malrotation and a duodenal web is exceptional, with only a few clinical cases reported in the literature. A full-term neonate presented with signs of intestinal obstruction. Chest and abdominal radiography and ultrasonography led to the diagnosis of situs inversus totalis and duodenal obstruction on the first day of life. An intraoperative contrast study confirmed intestinal malrotation. A Ladd procedure and resection of the duodenal web were performed through a supraumbilical midline approach. The patient had a favorable postoperative course. In neonates with situs inversus and intestinal obstruction, we suggest considering intestinal malrotation and a duodenal web as possible concomitant diagnoses. Imaging studies are essential for establishing the surgical strategy. El situs inversus totalis es una anomalía congénita de poca incidencia en el mundo (1 en 10.000 nacidos vivos). Su asociación a malrotación intestinal y membrana duodenal es excepcional y cuenta con pocos casos clínicos reportados en la literatura. Neonato a término con signos de oclusión intestinal. Con radiografía toracoabdominal y ecografía se diagnostica situs inversus totalis y obstrucción duodenal al primer día de vida. En quirófano se realiza estudio contrastado donde se confirma malrotación intestinal. Se realiza cirugía de Ladd y resección de membrana duodenal mediante abordaje mediano supraumbilical. El paciente evolucionó de forma favorable. En caso de neonatos con situs inversus y oclusión intestinal, sugerimos considerar la malrotación y la membrana duodenal como posibles diagnósticos concomitantes, para lo cual nos apoyamos en estudios de imagen siendo estos imprescindibles para la estrategia quirúrgica.

## Surgical Antibiotic Prophylaxis Utilization for Pediatric Thyroid Operations: A NSQIP-Pediatric Analysis.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:13
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 美国 · Center for Surgical Outcomes Research
- 通讯作者: Arnold EC (Erica.arnold@nationwidechildrens.org)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163324
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163324) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498071/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：了解儿童甲状腺手术中预防性抗生素的使用情况及其与手术部位感染的关系。  
- 核心发现/数据：在1040例清洁甲状腺手术中，45.1%接受了预防性抗生素，其中儿童耳鼻喉科（69.0%）和成人耳鼻喉科（48.7%）使用率显著高于儿童外科（30.3%）和普通外科（14.2%）。总体手术部位感染率为0.6%，所有感染均发生在未使用抗生素组（1.1%），且全部为全甲状腺切除术患者，其中83.3%患有甲状腺毒症，83.3%体重指数>85百分位。  
- 临床启示：尽管儿童甲状腺术后手术部位感染率极低，但对于因甲状腺毒症或肥胖接受全甲状腺切除术的患者，应谨慎考虑使用预防性抗生素。

**原文摘要**：Thyroid operations are generally clean procedures. However, there are no pediatric-specific guidelines regarding surgical antibiotic prophylaxis (SAP) for thyroid operations. This study aims to understand SAP utilization for thyroid operations in children and its association with surgical site infection (SSI). Patients <18 years old who underwent clean thyroid lobectomy and total thyroidectomy between 2021-2023 were identified in the National Surgical Quality Improvement Program-Pediatric (NSQIP-P) database. Patients with pre-existing infection and non-clean wound class were excluded. A single institutional cohort was also analyzed. SSI rates were compared between those who did and did not receive SAP. Among the 1,040 thyroid procedures, 45.1% received SAP. SAP was administered more commonly by pediatric (69.0%) and adult otolaryngologists (48.7%) compared to pediatric (30.3%) and general surgeons (14.2%, p<0.001). The SSI rate in the entire cohort was 0.6% (n=6). All SSIs occurred in the no-SAP group (1.1%, p=0.035 when compared to SAP group). All patients with SSI underwent total thyroidectomy. Of the six patients with SSI, 83.3% (n=5) had thyrotoxicosis, 83.3% (n=5) patients had BMI >85 SSI risk following pediatric thyroid operations is extremely low. Patients undergoing total thyroidectomy for thyrotoxicosis and those with BMI >85

## Primary Radical Surgery or Preoperative Drainage? A Clinical Decision Framework for Pediatric Choledochal Cysts.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:13
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 中国 · Children's Hospital of Nanjing Medical University
- 通讯作者: Fu Y (fyh1203@139.com)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163321
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163321) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498070/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：开发一个基于术前变量的客观风险分层框架，以指导儿童胆总管囊肿患者选择一期根治手术还是术前引流。  
- 核心发现/数据：一期根治手术患者严重并发症发生率为6.3%；主要预测因子为肝内胆管扩张（OR 3.58）和炎症评分≥2（OR 2.35）；基于炎症评分、肝内扩张和血红蛋白构建的11分简化评分将患者分为低（5.5%）、中（13.6%）、高（18.3%）风险组；术前引流患者若接受一期根治手术，预测不良结局风险显著高于实际一期根治手术患者（19.2% vs. 13.2%）。  
- 临床启示：该初步风险评分工具可辅助识别一期根治手术后预后不良风险较高的儿童，但需外部验证，且应作为临床判断的补充而非替代。

**原文摘要**：For children with complicated choledochal cyst (CDC), the decision between primary radical surgery (PRS) and preoperative drainage (PD) remains subjective. We aimed to develop an objective preoperative risk framework to inform this choice. A retrospective, single-center review of 454 children with CDCs was conducted. The primary outcome was disease-related severe complications (Clavien-Dindo ≥II). A prediction model for severe complications was built using logistic regression. A sensitivity analysis used a composite outcome that also included extreme surgical difficulty (prolonged operative time plus prolonged hospital stay). A point score was derived from the composite-outcome analysis. Severe complications occurred in 24 of 380 PRS patients (6.3%). Intrahepatic duct dilatation (OR 3.58, P=0.005) and inflammation score ≥2 (OR 2.35, P=0.058) were the main predictors. In the composite-outcome analysis (50 events), a simplified 11-point score (inflammation score, intrahepatic dilatation, hemoglobin) stratified patients into low- (0 points, 5.5%), medium- (1-3 points, 13.6%) and high-risk (≥4 points, 18.3%) groups. PD patients (n=74) had a higher predicted risk of suboptimal outcome if they had undergone PRS (19.2% vs. observed 13.2% in PRS, P<0.001). A preliminary risk score using three preoperative variables can help identify children at higher risk of poor outcome after PRS. The tool requires external validation and should support, not replace, clinical judgment. Level III TYPE OF STUDY: Diagnostic/Prognostic.

## Colonic Manometry and Contrast Enema Findings in Children With Ineffective Antegrade Continence Enema Therapy.

- 来源: JPS 圣刊 · 推送 09:13
- 期刊: J Pediatr Surg · 中科院医学2区(儿科1区/外科2区) · IF 2.3 · JCR Q2
- 国家/单位: 美国 · Cincinnati Children's Hospital Medical Center
- 通讯作者: Kaul A (ajay.kaul@cchmc.org)
- DOI: 10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163325
- 获取全文: [DOI 全文](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.jpedsurg.2026.163325) · [PubMed](https://pubmed.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/42498069/)

**中文总结**：- 研究目的：探讨儿童顺行灌肠（ACE）治疗失效的生理和解剖学相关因素。  
- 核心发现/数据：在22名ACE治疗无效的患儿中，41%存在结肠测压异常；与测压正常者相比，异常组的总高幅推进性收缩波、比沙可啶诱导的高幅推进性收缩波及右结肠收缩幅度均显著更低（均p≤0.01），但结肠直径无显著差异且与测压结果不相关（右：r=0.04，p=0.87；左：r=-0.12，p=0.57）。ACE使用≥36个月的患儿右结肠扩张比例更高（83.3% vs 30%，p=0.027），最大右结肠直径更大（p=0.02），且右结肠收缩幅度低于左结肠（p=0.02）。  
- 临床启示：长期ACE使用（≥36个月）与右结肠扩张及选择性结肠动力参数改变相关，提示对ACE失效患儿应行结肠测压和造影检查以评估结肠功能与结构异常，指导治疗调整。

**原文摘要**：Antegrade continence enema (ACE) is an effective therapy for many children with medically refractory constipation. However, some patients lose response over time. This study aims to describe and explore physiological and anatomic correlates of ACE failure in children. Electronic medical records of children who were no longer responding to ACE and were seen in our collaborative neurogastroenterology and colorectal surgery clinic over 7 years were reviewed. Demographic and clinical data, as well as contrast enema (CE) and colonic manometry (CM) findings, were examined in all subjects. Data was compared among the cohort who had a normal versus abnormal CM study, and between those who had a duration of ACE use<36 months versus >36 months. Over a 7-year period, 22 children (median age 11 years, IQR 8.1-14.4; 54.5% male) with ineffective ACE therapy were included, of whom 41% had abnormal colonic manometry. Patients with normal CM demonstrated significantly greater total and bisacodyl-induced high-amplitude propagated contractions and higher right colonic contractile amplitude compared with those with abnormal manometry (all p ≤ 0.01), but colonic caliber did not differ and did not correlate with manometry findings (right: r = 0.04, p = 0.87; left: r = -0.12, p = 0.57). Patients with ACE duration ≥36 months had a higher prevalence of right colonic dilation (83.3% vs 30%, p = 0.027) and larger maximal right colonic diameter (p = 0.02). Within this group, right colonic contractile amplitude was lower than left colonic contractile amplitude (p = 0.02). Prolonged ACE use (≥36 months) demonstrated a higher prevalence of right colonic dilation and differences in selected colonic manometry parameters compared with those with shorter ACE duration.
